The residual head constructs ordered traffic-risk environments and optimizes horizon-wise flow corrections with a risk extrapolation objective, thereby mitigating regime-specific shortcut correlations without modifying the backbone. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RiskTraf consistently improves diverse forecasting backbones and outperforms debiasing and distribution-shift adaptation methods. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/RiskTraf.

</details>


### [147] [Lightweight Adaptive ReduNet via Hyperspherical Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20668)
*Zhenglin Huang,Qifa Yan,Bin Dai,Xiaohu Tang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出LA-ReduNet，通过超球面流形学习和自适应步长，将ReduNet达到MCR²目标稳定所需的层数减少一个数量级，参数存储降至约1/29，同时保持分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReduNet虽具可解释性，但逐层构造需大量层数才能稳定MCR²目标，导致参数存储过大，限制其实际应用。

Method: 在ReduNet的逐层更新规则中引入超球面流形学习（约束特征在单位超球面上以增强判别性）和自适应步长（动态调整更新幅度以加速收敛），从而减少所需层数。

Result: 仿真显示，LA-ReduNet在保持分类精度相当的同时，显著减少MCR²目标稳定所需层数，参数存储约为原ReduNet的1/29。

Conclusion: LA-ReduNet有效缓解了ReduNet的层数冗余问题，验证了轻量化自适应架构在高维判别特征提取中的可行性。

Abstract: In recent years, a white-box neural network called ReduNet has been proposed, which employs the maximal coding rate reduction (MCR$^2$) principle to transform raw data into low-dimensional discriminative features via a forward layer-wise construction process. Unlike traditional deep networks that rely on backpropagation, ReduNet explicitly derives the parameters of each layer from the features of its preceding layer, offering a mathematically interpretable paradigm. However, this layer-wise construction often requires a large number of layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value, which increases the parameter storage of the unfolded module. To address this issue, we propose LA-ReduNet, a lightweight adaptive architecture that refines the layer-wise update rule and enables discriminative feature representations to be obtained with substantially fewer unfolded layers. Specifically, LA-ReduNet employs hyperspherical manifold learning and adaptive step sizes, thereby reducing by an order of magnitude the number of layers required for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Simulation results demonstrate that, while maintaining comparable classification accuracy, LA-ReduNet requires significantly fewer layers for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value. Remarkably, under the considered experimental settings, LA-ReduNet requires only approximately $1/29$ of the parameter storage of the unfolded ReduNet module for the MCR$^2$ objective to reach a stable value.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Effectiveness: A Multi-Criteria Framework for Comparing Practical Socio-Technical Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20649)
*Catherine King,Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Kathleen M. Carley*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多标准评估框架，用于在社会技术干预措施（如错误信息治理）中比较其政治可行性、有效性、用户接受度、成本和实施难度，并发现专家认为最有效的措施往往不是最可行或最被接受的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会技术领域的决策者缺乏一种基于原则的方法来比较各种干预措施，现有研究多侧重于效果评估，而忽略成本、可行性等实施约束。

Method: 通过对39名研究人员进行调查，针对40种错误信息干预措施，从政治可行性、有效性、用户接受度、成本和实施努力五个标准进行评分，并分析标准间的权衡关系。

Result: 专家判断的最有效干预措施并非总是最受公众接受或最易实施的，表明效果与可行性之间存在张力。

Conclusion: 该框架为实践者提供了决策支持，帮助他们在多标准下权衡社会技术干预措施，适用范围可超越错误信息领域。

Abstract: Designers and policymakers in sociotechnical domains like content moderation, privacy interfaces, recommender systems and beyond, must choose among a growing menu of proposed interventions, but typically lack a principled basis for comparing them. Prior work tends to evaluate interventions individually and mostly along the effectiveness criteria, while implementation constraints such as cost, effort and feasibility are often considered separately. We present a multi-criteria framework for evaluating sociotechnical interventions. This framework is instantiated through the case of misinformation, a domain of intense focus for proposed countermeasures. We survey $N=39$ researchers on 40 operationalized interventions across five evaluative criteria: political feasibility, effectiveness, user acceptance, cost, and implementation effort. We find that the interventions that experts judge to be the most effective are not always the most acceptable to the public or the most feasible to implement. We also discuss how this tension has implications for the design of sociotechnical interventions beyond misinformation, and offer a decision framework for practitioners navigating the trade-offs of sociotechnical interventions.

</details>


### [149] [Fuzzy-MoE: Interpretable Regime-Conditioned Expert Routing for Non-Stationary Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20761)
*Lan Guo,Jie Xiao,Zhao Su,Jun Shen,Haoran Li,Weixia Ma,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Fuzzy-MoE，通过模糊逻辑路由将时间序列预测重构为潜在状态识别与可解释专家路由的统一框架，在多个基准上提升精度并提供透明路由诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳多变量时间序列中变量和样本常具备异质潜在动态状态，现有深度预测模型将其压缩为统一端到端映射，导致对时变动态建模次优，且缺乏对不同潜在状态下预测机制的可解释性。

Method: Fuzzy-MoE包含多个并行专家映射网络和双视图模糊路由器；路由器结合局部卷积动态和全局分段统计推断潜在时间状态，通过可学习高斯隶属函数计算专家激活强度，实现基于IF-THEN规则的显式专家选择，支持同一序列内不同变量激活不同专家。

Result: 在多个公开时间序列基准数据集上，Fuzzy-MoE在预测精度上显著优于主流预测方法，同时模糊隶属度和规则激活提供可解释的路由诊断。

Conclusion: 相比传统黑盒路由MoE，Fuzzy-MoE基于清晰可解释的模糊规则，使专家选择透明可追溯，在预测性能和机制透明度上均验证了框架有效性。

Abstract: In non-stationary multivariate time series, different variables and samples often exhibit heterogeneous latent dynamic states, while existing deep forecasting models usually compress them into a unified end-to-end mapping, leading to suboptimal modeling of time-varying dynamics and limited interpretability regarding which forecasting mechanism is activated under different latent states. To overcome these limitations, we reformulate time series forecasting as a unified framework of latent temporal state identification and interpretable expert routing, and propose Fuzzy-MoE, a fuzzy logic-based dynamic Mixture-of-Experts model. Fuzzy-MoE consists of multiple parallel expert mapping networks and a dual-view fuzzy router. By jointly exploiting local convolutional dynamics and global segmented statistics, the router infers latent temporal states and computes expert activation strengths through learnable Gaussian membership functions, enabling explicit IF-THEN rule-based expert selection. This fine-grained routing strategy allows different variables within the same sequence to activate different experts, effectively capturing heterogeneous temporal dynamics while improving model interpretability. Experimental results on multiple public time series benchmark datasets show that Fuzzy-MoE significantly outperforms mainstream forecasting methods in forecasting accuracy. Moreover, fuzzy memberships and rule activations provide interpretable routing diagnostics, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in both forecasting performance and mechanism transparency. Unlike traditional MoE models that use black-box routing, Fuzzy-MoE`s routing is based on clear, interpretable fuzzy rules. This makes the expert selection transparent and traceable.

</details>


### [150] [Resolution-Consistent Greedy Neural Approximation on Infinite-Dimensional Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20812)
*Pablo M. Berná,Antonio Falcó,Diego Mondéjar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文针对无限维输入经有限坐标观测的浅层神经模型，建立了构造性逼近与学习保证，揭示误差在分布相关的截断项与贪心有限宽度项间分离，并给出统计复杂度随输入分辨率均匀的回归保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论多聚焦有限维输入或仅处理逼近性质，缺乏对无限维输入经有限观测时逼近与学习误差的系统刻画，且需厘清维度无关保证的统计与计算边界。

Method: 基于参数归一化神经字典及其加权变差类，将逼近误差分解为坐标截断项与贪心宽度项；采用完全修正贪心程序分析经验回归，并扩展至希尔伯特值响应；借助拟波兰空间构造提供具体示例与合成实验。

Result: 获得统计维度无关的总体风险保证，且不显式依赖输出维度；实验验证了分辨率、宽度与样本量的预测区间；揭示统计可处理性不意味着计算可处理性——新神经元选择仍是非凸搜索。

Conclusion: 在无限维输入浅层模型中，维度无关学习保证在统计意义下成立，但计算瓶颈依然存在；截断误差与贪心误差的分解为设计高效算法提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We develop constructive approximation and learning guarantees for shallow neural models with infinite-dimensional inputs observed through finitely many coordinates. The analysis is based on a parameter-normalized neural dictionary and its associated weighted variation class. Within this class, the approximation error separates into a distribution-dependent coordinate-truncation term and a greedy finite-width term. For empirical regression, a fully-corrective greedy procedure yields population guarantees whose statistical complexity is uniform in the retained input resolution. The same framework extends to Hilbert-valued responses without an explicit dependence on the output dimension. The dimension-free statements are statistical, not computational: selecting a new neuron still requires solving a nonconvex parameter-search problem. The quasi-Polish construction underlying recent infinite-dimensional universal approximation results provides a motivating example, and synthetic experiments illustrate the predicted resolution, width, and sample-size regimes.

</details>


### [151] [VortexChat: An agentic framework for autonomous multi-objective integrated photonic design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20688)
*Faqian Chong,Yulun Wu,Shilong Li,Andrew Forbes,Hongsheng Chen,Song Han*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出VortexChat，一个基于大语言模型的智能体框架，实现从自然语言规格到集成光子器件逆设计的全自动端到端流程，并在太赫兹涡旋光束复用器上完成实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光子器件设计依赖人工仿真与专家直觉，逆向设计也需专家监督，缺乏端到端自动化，难以应对现代集成光子学的复杂设计瓶颈。

Method: 将LLM决策智能体与拓扑生成、梯度优化和全波电磁仿真耦合，形成闭环架构，自主分解设计目标、调用计算工具并根据反馈迭代策略，无需人工介入。

Result: 在Vortex100基准约束下，自主生成的器件严格满足所有性能阈值；实验制备的宽带太赫兹完美涡旋光束复用器实测高效率、高模式纯度、低串扰，与仿真一致。

Conclusion: LLM智能体能承担光子逆向设计中关键专家决策，同时保持物理保真度和可制造性，为复杂集成光子系统的自主设计提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: The advancement of modern integrated photonics is frequently bottlenecked by device design workflows that rely heavily on manual simulation and expert intuition. While inverse design offers an alternative, it remains constrained by expert supervision and a lack of end-to-end automation. To address these issues, we present VortexChat, an agentic framework for the autonomous, end-to-end inverse design of integrated photonic devices directly from natural language specifications. VortexChat couples a large language model (LLM) decision agent with topology generation, gradient-based refinement, and full-wave electromagnetic simulation. This closed-loop architecture enables the system to iteratively decompose design objectives, orchestrate computational tools, and update strategies based on feedback with minimal human intervention. Constrained by the absolute metrics of the Vortex100 Benchmark, VortexChat autonomously generates devices that strictly meet all predefined performance thresholds without any human-in-the-loop. As an experimental demonstration, we fabricated a broadband terahertz perfect vortex beam multiplexer, autonomously designed by VortexChat, with measurements confirming high-efficiency operation, high mode purity and low inter-channel crosstalk in agreement with full-wave simulations. These results demonstrate that an LLM agent can assume key aspects of expert decision-making in photonic inverse design while maintaining physical fidelity and fabrication feasibility, providing a scalable route towards autonomous design of complex integrated photonic systems.

</details>


### [152] [A Critical Audit of Spatiotemporal Forecasting Benchmark Datasets and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20980)
*Kenneth Martin,Simon Heilig,Asja Fischer,Michel F. C. Haddad,Adam M. Sykulski,Moshe Eliasof*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过经典时间序列方法分析现有GNN基准数据集，揭示空间无关线性模型竞争力被低估的原因，并发现一阶差分数据集的结构性偏差，建议减少对现有数据集的依赖并加强统计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN短时预测研究过度依赖少数基准数据集（如水痘、METR-LA等），且评估协议中的经典基线往往表现与GNN相当，但缺乏对数据集内在统计特性的深入剖析，导致方法比较的判别力存疑。

Method: 采用经典时间序列统计工具（如相关性分析、差分处理等）系统检验基准数据集中的空间与时间依赖性，识别一阶差分引入的结构性偏差，并据此设计混合模型验证分析结论。

Result: 发现空间无关线性模型在多数数据集上表现优于现有报道，且一阶差分数据集存在人为增强短期相关性的偏差，削弱了GNN对比评估的可靠性。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区减少对现有基准数据集的过度依赖，采用更严格的统计检验来评估模型，并展示了基于统计分析结果构建混合模型的新思路，可为GNN设计提供指导。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [153] [Natural-Language-Guided Generator-Agnostic Shortlisting for Protein Binder Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20755)
*Gyubok Lee,Kiwoong Yoo,Jimin Seo,Kyunghoon Hur,Edward Choi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 探索LLM生成排序策略用于蛋白质候选结合剂筛选，以可解释方式组合多个代理分数，性能小幅优于单一特征基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验验证瓶颈限制蛋白质结合剂筛选效率，需要更智能的多指标组合排序方法。

Method: 利用LLM迭代生成全局或目标条件化的排序策略，基于预计算的代理分数对候选池进行排序，无需修改设计流程。

Result: LLM策略在多个数据集上达到或超越最强单特征基线，目标条件化策略表现更优。

Conclusion: LLM生成的排序策略可作为有效的后期筛选工具，提升结合剂选择效率并保持可解释性。

Abstract: Modern de novo design workflows generate many candidate protein binders, but wet-lab validation capacity remains limited, making shortlisting a major bottleneck. We study whether LLMs can generate multi-metric ranking policies from precomputed structural-confidence and interface-quality proxy scores. Rather than proposing a new protein binder design pipeline, we focus on post-generation binder shortlisting: selecting the final top-K candidates from already generated binder pools using a shared panel of precomputed proxy scores. On the 10-target held-out split, averaging performance over five sampled global iterative gpt-4o policies reaches 0.589 Recall@10, modestly improving over the strongest single-feature fixed baseline, Protenix binder ipTM, which reaches 0.571 Recall@10. On the 3-target held-out subset comprising Nipah, RBX1, and TREM2, target-conditioned iterative gpt-5.4 policies reach the strongest LLM performance, with 0.519 Recall@10 and 0.583 NDCG@10. These results suggest that LLM-generated ranking policies can act as an interpretable post-generation decision layer for combining heterogeneous proxy metrics to prioritize binders from large candidate pools.

</details>


### [154] [Causal Modeling of Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes via Adaptive LLM Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21079)
*Kavimayil P. Komarasamy,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,David M. Haas,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号框架，迭代结合LLM先验知识与数据经验评分，用于生成不良妊娠结局的因果假设，在真实临床数据上恢复了专家验证的所有边并发现新关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不良妊娠结局（如早产、妊娠糖尿病）对母婴有长期影响，但其成因难以理解；该领域数据稀缺、领域知识不完整，纯数据驱动方法失效，而LLM输出不一致或矛盾，需要新方法来生成可靠的因果假设。

Method: 将LLM作为自适应提议分布，生成因果假设图，用经验数据评分，将高分图反馈到LLM上下文中，引导后续生成朝向更有希望的假设空间区域，迭代进行。

Result: 在真实临床数据集上评估，方法恢复了专家构建的因果图中的所有专家验证边，并识别出专家未列出的额外合理因果关系，可能为靶向干预提供新见解。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架能有效组合LLM知识与数据证据，生成更全面且合理的因果假设，优于纯数据驱动或纯LLM方法，有望提升对APOs病因的理解和干预设计。

Abstract: Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes (APOs) such as preterm birth and gestational diabetes can have long-term consequences for both the mother and child, yet an understanding of their causes remains elusive. Causal discovery in this domain is especially challenging due to a paucity of data and incomplete domain knowledge. As a result, pure data-driven methods fail, and Large Language Model (LLM) outputs remain inconsistent or contradictory. We introduce a neurosymbolic framework for generating plausible causal hypotheses that iteratively combines the broad prior knowledge of LLMs with empirical scoring on data. Our method treats the LLM as an adaptive proposal distribution, generating hypotheses that are scored against empirical data; the resulting high-scoring graphs are then used to update the LLM's context, steering subsequent generations toward more promising regions of the hypothesis space. We evaluate our approach on a real-world clinical dataset for modeling APOs and their risk factors, comparing our results against an expert-constructed causal graph. Our method recovers all expert-validated edges and identifies additional plausible causal relations not previously listed by experts, potentially providing new insights for targeted interventions.

</details>


### [155] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Winder，一种利用心脏周期相移等变性的自监督表征学习方法，以无参数固定传输算子组织潜空间，在PTB-XL上以~1M参数达到SOTA诊断精度，并保持可解释的相位几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的循环结构为自监督学习提供天然先验，心脏周期尤其适合；现有方法未显式编码相位对称性，导致潜空间不可解释且参数效率低。

Method: 推导相位等变自监督目标，设计联合嵌入架构Winder，将表征分解为相位不变坐标和相位旋转谐波子空间；传输算子由循环几何解析确定，固定且无参数。

Result: 在PTB-XL冻结线性探针协议下，Winder以约1M参数取得与SOTA自监督方法相当的诊断准确率，并呈现相位等变潜几何。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性能在保持诊断信息的同时，实现可读、参数高效且与生理量直接关联的潜空间，为生理信号自监督学习提供新范式。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [156] [Neuro-Geospatial Modelling of EEG Affective States Using Literature-Informed Environmental Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20807)
*Utsav Poudel,Jagannath Aryal,Subramaniyaswamy Vairavasundaram*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将EEG与环境先验（地理数据）融合的双塔架构，在EEG情感分类中达到76.2%准确率（EEG单独为67.4%），但对照实验表明增益并非完全来自环境信息，且环境分布替换会降低性能，说明该方法具技术可行性但未证明因果关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境污染和绿化等环境因素可能与情感和认知相关，但EEG与环境数据很少联合地理参考，且个体级暴露数据常不可得，因此探索将文献提供的环境先验作为辅助地理模态用于EEG情感分类。

Method: 使用EAV基准的30通道EEG数据（42名参与者），结合Astana的OpenAQ、Sentinel-2、Sentinel-5P和OpenStreetMap数据构建环境表征；采用双塔架构：EEG用EEG-Conformer，环境用图编码器；因数据未联合注册，环境上下文作为文献先验而非测量暴露；通过多次重复划分、置换/标签混淆对照、剂量-反应反转和域迁移实验来区分架构增益与先验依赖增益。

Result: 多模态模型准确率76.2%，EEG单独67.4%；破坏环境标签结构的对照保留部分增益，表明增益不单由环境信息贡献；将Astana环境分布替换为新加坡独立模型数据后准确率降至72.8%。

Conclusion: 研究证明技术可行性，但未确立环境暴露与情感间的观测或因果关联，为未来联合移动EEG-环境研究提供框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Environmental exposures such as air pollution and greenness have been associated with affective and cognitive outcomes, but EEG and environmental datasets are rarely jointly georeferenced. We investigate whether literature-informed environmental priors can serve as an auxiliary geospatial modality for EEG-based affective-state classification when individual-level exposure data are unavailable. We combine 30-channel EEG from the EAV benchmark (42 participants, aged 20-30 years) with environmental representations derived from OpenAQ, Sentinel-2, Sentinel-5P, and OpenStreetMap data for Astana. A dual-tower architecture combines EEG-Conformer representations with a graph-based environmental encoder. Because the datasets are not co-registered, environmental context is treated as a literature-informed prior rather than measured exposure. Subject-level repeated splits, permutation and label-shuffling controls, dose-response reversal, and domain-shift experiments distinguish architecture-level gains from prior-dependent gains. The multimodal model achieves 76.2% accuracy versus 67.4% for EEG alone. Controls disrupting environmental-label structure retain part of this gain, indicating that the improvement is not attributable solely to environmental information. Replacing the Astana environmental distribution with an independently modeled Singapore distribution reduces accuracy to 72.8%. These findings demonstrate technical feasibility but do not establish an observed or causal exposure-affect association. The study provides a framework for future jointly collected mobile EEG-environment studies. Implementation: https://github.com/r11up/geo-cog

</details>


### [157] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: TRACE-C是一种针对多流遥测数据的可审计严格先验秩校准异常检测器，在2020年冻结数据上未选出事件，但作者认为这是记录规则饱和而非年份平静，且其排名结果与通道消融分析存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法在单个流正常但联合异常时失效，且缺乏可审计性和严格的先验概率校准，导致检测结果难以解释和验证。

Method: 使用滚动中位数/MAD残差，结合三个窗口通道（最大归一化局部和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、最差标准化AR(1)创新），将通道秩通过Fisher聚合后进行排序，并公开通道消融分析。

Result: 在GB电网六个流上，2019年测试中风暴Atiyah排名第一但归因于局部通道而非copula通道；短时频率事件融合排名低于时间通道单用；2020年无窗口选出，最高排名的冻结窗口后被解释为风暴Ellen。

Conclusion: 该方法提供严格先验校准和可审计性，但p值为选择量而非事件概率，copula通道非字面copula密度，经验秩计数不能证明覆盖率或FDR，需谨慎解读结果。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [158] [MGAL: A Multilingual Granularity-Aware Long-Context Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20853)
*Chunhan Li,Chenglin Xu,Zongyang Zhang,Jiale Liu,Zhuoxi Rao,Xudong Jia,Junxiu He,Menglin Yang,Wenjuan Gong,Zhengzhe Liu,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出首个多语言、粒度与位置感知的长上下文基准MGAL，发现LLM在粗粒度任务和低资源语言上表现较差，并识别出局部语义拥挤和生成流畅性与一致性差距等新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文基准多局限于文档级别且聚焦高资源语言，缺乏对细粒度挑战的多语言评估。

Method: 基于联合国报告构建覆盖六种官方语言、8K-128K token的基准，包含词、句、段、文档四个粒度层级，并按文档内位置（开头、中间、结尾）及文档/段落级索引分层。

Result: 实验发现：词级任务表现好而粗粒度任务差；闭源模型在低资源语言上仍有优势；新发现局部语义拥挤导致依赖表层线索、生成流畅性与事实一致性脱节等问题。

Conclusion: MGAL为系统诊断多语言长上下文理解提供了细粒度工具，揭示了模型在粗粒度推理和低资源语言上的明显短板及新挑战。

Abstract: Evaluation of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced rapidly. However, most existing benchmarks are limited to the document level and focus mainly on high-resource languages, leaving many fine-grained challenges insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we present MGAL, the first multilingual, granularity- and position-aware long-context benchmark. MGAL is constructed from United Nations (UN) reports spanning 8K to 128K tokens across the six official UN languages. It covers four coherent levels of linguistic granularity (word, sentence, paragraph, and document) and further stratifies entries by their position within the document (begin, middle, and end), indexed at both the document and paragraph levels. This design enables systematic diagnosis of multilingual long-context comprehension across different granularities.
  Through extensive experiments and analyses, we find that: (1) LLMs perform well at word-level tasks but struggle with coarser-grained ones; and (2) Closed-source models retain a clear performance advantage in lower-resource languages. We further identify two new challenges: (1) Under local semantic crowding, where neighboring sentences share topics and entities, models tend to follow surface cues (e.g., connectives like ``however'' or repeated entities) rather than the discourse role of the sentence in surrounding context (e.g., background, outcome); and (2) A gap between fluency and consistency in generated outputs, where models produce text that reads smoothly but drifts from the source facts. In addition, we observe several patterns in line with prior studies, including reliance on nearby evidence and reuse of options under uncertainty.

</details>


### [159] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: SPARCL通过频谱分区冻结旧类核心子空间，缓解解析持续学习中的频谱干扰，在多个基准上显著提升旧类稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析持续学习虽用闭式岭回归避免梯度覆盖，但仍有旧类漂移；原因在于共享自相关逆算子的谱干扰，而非传统梯度覆盖。

Method: 将自相关矩阵分解为高能核心和残差部分，冻结核心子空间中的旧类分类器，仅用递归最小二乘更新残差块，并可选随机投影扩展残差。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上（冻结ViT-B/16），SPARCL缩小了与强表示匹配方法的差距，并与Fly-CL互补。

Conclusion: 解析持续学习可通过显式谱分区实现旧类不变性，为类增量学习提供更稳定且高效的闭式更新方案。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [160] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对慢阻肺急性加重（AECOPD）预测延迟问题，提出基于时间感知Transformer的模型，仅利用家用呼吸机呼吸数据，在多项分类任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AECOPD预测模型依赖临床和实验室数据，存在固有延迟，难以满足急性加重快速症状变化对及时预测的需求。

Method: 构建时间感知Transformer模型，通过时间感知机制编码呼吸机数据中的症状及其时间进展，生成患者表征并用于AECOPD预测。

Result: 实验表明，时间感知Transformer方法在多个分类任务上优于传统方法，显著提升AECOPD预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该模型有望在家庭监测场景下实现更及时的AECOPD预警，减少医疗延迟，改善患者管理。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [161] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏定价面板数据中，估计误差的变异主要来源于跨设计（不同价格轨迹）而非给定轨迹内的条件不确定性，基线模拟中跨设计成分占梯度提升模型误差方差的97.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短观测定价面板虽包含大量观测，但价格变动次数很少，导致推断结果可能严重依赖于特定的价格轨迹，而传统重采样方法无法捕捉这种跨设计的变异性。

Method: 基于校准到稀疏定价机制的综合数据生成过程，分离给定价格轨迹条件下的不确定性与其他轨迹引起的估计误差变异，并引入Paule-Mandel方差分量以量化跨独立定价单元的异质性。

Result: 跨设计离散度满足σ̂≈0.182V^(-0.271)；共享价格路径的区域不产生独立轨迹，而独立设计误差的单元均值以平方根速率降低离散度；Paule-Mandel方法使同质模拟中的经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。

Conclusion: 应转向设计能产生独立识别变动的数据生成过程，而非仅依赖固定被动面板，以提高推断的稳健性和覆盖概率。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [162] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首次对自精炼流程中的生成、批判、修订三个阶段进行模型规模影响的系统研究，发现生成器和修订器越大越好，但批判器规模对性能影响极小，且即使小批判器也有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自精炼方法将模型大小视为实现细节而非研究对象，缺乏对生成、批判、修订各阶段模型规模需求的系统分析，可能导致资源浪费。

Method: 在5个不同领域基准上，使用Qwen3的6种规模和Gemma 3的4种规模，对自精炼流水线进行逐阶段模型规模对比实验。

Result: 较大的生成器和修订器通常提升整体性能，而过小的修订器可能损害性能；批判器规模对性能高度不敏感，但省略批判环节会使性能下降。

Conclusion: 自精炼流水线各阶段对模型容量需求不同，不应均匀分配计算资源，该发现为设计高效多阶段语言模型系统提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


### [163] [Can Scientific Claims Be Removed from Large Language Models? A Systematic Evaluation of Claim-Level Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20960)
*Snigdha Paul,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出科学声明遗忘任务及基准SciUnlearn，发现现有机器遗忘方法无法有效删除声明级知识，仅实现表面抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学知识不断更新，语言模型可能传播过时或已撤回的声明，现有遗忘研究多聚焦实例级遗忘，缺乏针对相互关联且动态演化的科学声明的专门方法。

Method: 构建科学声明遗忘基准SciUnlearn，系统评估现有遗忘方法在声明级知识删除上的表现，分析其浅层抑制现象。

Result: 现有遗忘方法无法彻底消除声明级知识，仅表面隐藏而非真正遗忘，表明需设计结构化知识删除的专用方法。

Conclusion: 强调开发针对科学声明复杂特性的专用遗忘技术的必要性，以支持科学工作流中的知识时效性。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are trained on static scientific corpora, whereas scientific knowledge continuously evolves through correction and revision. Scientific claims encoded within these models may later become retracted, disproven, or updated by subsequent research, creating the risk of disseminating outdated information in scientific workflows. This creates a need for LMs to forget obsolete scientific claims. Machine unlearning offers a promising solution by enabling knowledge removal while maintaining overall model utility. Existing studies primarily investigate instance-level forgetting; however, scientific claims introduce additional challenges because they are interconnected, and continually evolving. To address this gap, we introduce the task of Scientific Claim Unlearning and present a new benchmark, SciUnlearn. We show that current unlearning approaches are unable to effectively eliminate claim-level knowledge and often achieve only superficial suppression, highlighting the need for specialized methods designed for structured knowledge removal.

</details>


### [164] [Deep Learning Models Also Recall Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20970)
*Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出‘特征召回’概念，认为线性投影可理解为按输入激活缩放检索存储信息，并将其与特征组合范式对比，为深度学习的机制解释提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性研究聚焦大语言模型的事实召回，但作者认为这指向更普适的深层操作，即特征召回，有必要对此进行系统定义与跨架构论证。

Method: 通过概念分析定义特征召回，展示其跨架构适用性，并与特征组合范式对比，同时探讨机制性识别的可能途径。

Result: 论证了特征召回作为通用操作的有效性，并指出其在哲学概念工具与实证研究方向上的双重意义。

Conclusion: 特征召回为理解深度学习提供新概念工具，并为机制可解释性研究指明实证方向。

Abstract: Recent work in mechanistic interpretability has studied how large language models recall facts stored in their weights. This paper argues that factual recall points to something broader: a general kind of operation in deep learning models, which I call feature recall. The core observation is that a linear projection can be read as retrieving stored information scaled by input activations. I define feature recall, show it applies across architectures, and contrast it with the established paradigm of feature combination. I also consider how cases of feature recall might be mechanistically identified. The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [165] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 形状约束与物理信息学习的精度代价主要源于自由特征和验证集划分，而非先验本身；通过嵌套约束类可导出无需凸性假设的代价上界，且代价与合规性相互独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将形状约束的精度代价归因于先验本身，本文旨在证明该代价实际由自由特征和验证协议主导，并提出可检验的边界以识别不可辨识的实验。

Method: 基于常数函数同时满足非增与非减约束的嵌套性质，证明受限类包含消融类，从而导出0≤P≤D的确定性上界；在野火严重度任务上采用硬单调约束、空间分块验证阶梯及两拟合筛查。

Result: 坐标作为自由特征恢复92.9%性能，将D从0.1288降至0.0427；受屏蔽代价0.0473与未屏蔽0.3470相差7.3倍；分块粒度改变导致D从0.0942降至0.0050；318次比较给出自我校准下限0.0220宏观F1；未约束模型先验违犯率0.48-0.49。

Conclusion: 形状约束代价是验证协议依赖的，不具可转移性；存在不可辨识的配置，需先验筛查避免无效训练；代价与合规性解耦，可独立优化。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [166] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CellPath-Bench，首个系统评估病理基础模型（PFM）在单细胞分辨率下细胞类型信息解码能力与跨域迁移能力的基准，涉及25个H&E-Xenium配对切片、11个器官、700余万细胞，通过30个模型和30万次实验揭示了模型间显著的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型基准无法系统诊断其在整张切片（WSI）水平上的细胞表示能力，特别是细胞类型信息是否可解码以及该信息能否跨切片、数据集和器官迁移，缺乏标准化评估工具。

Method: 构建25个空间对齐的H&E-Xenium组织切片数据集，统一细胞分类体系；在细胞核坐标处采样冻结WSI特征图，使用线性探针评估；提出CRA指标衡量核锚定表示相对patch均值池化的优势，CRT指标量化跨域可解码性迁移；通过30个模型和304,920次运行系统比较。

Result: 发现不同模型在细胞类型可解码性和跨域泛化上存在显著差异，形成不同的多维能力画像，说明细胞级表示能力与模型架构和训练策略高度相关。

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench为冻结病理基础模型的细胞信息审计提供了标准化框架，可指导模型选择和改进，但研究局限于现有Xenium数据集和线性探针协议，未来可扩展至更多组织类型和任务。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [167] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文挑战了LLM-as-Judge系统中信任评分与真值判定作为独立证据的假设，证明二者高度耦合且易受来源线索干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测系统常将多维度评估结果（如信任度、真值性）视为相互独立的证据，但这一假设尚未被严格验证，可能导致评测结论不可靠。

Method: 采用正确性受控的问答数据集，对比LLM法官与人类行为参考在信任评分和二元真值分类间的一致性；并通过仅改变同一问答的来源线索（人类/AI归属）进行压力测试，观察信任分数、真值判定及logit置信度变化。

Result: LLM法官的信任评分与真值判定紧密对齐，且来源线索改变会同时影响信任分数、真值判定和正确侧概率，说明两者并非独立。

Conclusion: 现有LLM-as-Judge协议不应将信任评分视为真值判定的独立证据来源，需重新设计评估框架以解耦不同维度。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [168] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文通过差分图发现方法，在线性时间序列中定位异常根因，重点识别因果系数在正常与异常状态间变化的变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法多依赖观测相关性或干预实验，难以在动态系统中准确识别机制性异常；差分图发现虽适用于两群体对比，但未扩展至时间序列场景。

Method: 基于线性离散时间动态结构因果模型，将正常与异常状态视为两个‘群体’，适配多种差分图发现算法至时间序列，并比较其性能。

Result: 模拟数据验证了方法有效性，IT监控和重症监护数据集上成功定位异常相关因果机制，表明差分图发现可实用化。

Conclusion: 差分图发现为时间序列根因分析提供了新视角，能有效分离机制性异常与系统性偏移，但需进一步优化计算效率与处理非线性场景。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [169] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM生成合规文档（如DPP和DPIA）质量和一致性的影响，发现格式要求严格时LLM输出稳定但易产生幻觉，格式宽松时需更高上下文提示以保持一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟可持续和隐私法规（如ESPR和GDPR）要求创建合规文档，但工业数据异构分散且DPIA缺乏标准化，导致人工编制困难。虽然已有研究提出用LLM辅助生成，但数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM输出质量的系统性影响尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 通过设计实验，比较不同LLM在两种法规场景（严格的DPP格式和宽松的DPIA格式）下，针对不同上下文提示生成合规文档的表现，并以人工构建的ground-truth schema为基准，评估输出的一致性和完整性。

Result: 对于严格格式（如数字电池护照），LLM输出一致性高但可能出现更多幻觉；对于宽松格式（如DPIA），需要更高上下文提示才能维持一致性和完整性，否则输出差异显著。

Conclusion: 法规格式的严格程度显著影响LLM生成合规文档的可靠性，提示策略需根据法规要求动态调整：格式宽松时增加上下文，格式严格时需警惕幻觉风险。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [170] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出 Steiner-TSP 在凸集图上的形式化，并设计统一的分支定界搜索算法，在无限解空间中保证有限终止与ε最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理允许重复访问与可选中转顶点的最小代价闭环问题，且连续轨迹与离散路径耦合导致解空间无限，缺乏统一且可证明有效的最优化框架。

Method: 建立根行走前缀的搜索树；用加性下界图约束前缀，用割分离连通流松弛估算剩余代价；在均匀正代价假设下，证明最佳优先搜索有限终止，深度优先在初始可行解下也有限；通过全局下界实现ε最优性证书，并集成LTL_f动作前序约束。

Result: 两种搜索策略在30秒内解决全部基准实例，平均最优性差距为28.1%和29.7%，而两个最新基线仅约半数实例成功。

Conclusion: 所提搜索框架在复杂检查任务中同时优化感知模式、访问顺序与连续轨迹，且性能与可证明性均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>
